  5-4- Liner Cementing Procedure

    1- Clean up tanks and preparing chemicals for mixing fluid.

    2-  Fill tanks with fresh water for mixing fluids (Lead and Tail)

    3- Before finished running Liner & 5" Drill Pipe with Co. man approval, start mixing cement

       additives and preparing mixing fluids according to below cement mixing procedure

         a. Add Antifoam to fresh water and allow mixing for 5 minutes.

        b. Add Fluid Loss Controller and allow mixing for 10 minutes.

         c. Add Dispersant and allow mixing for 10 minutes.

        d. Add High Temperature Retarder and allow mixing for 10 minutes.

         e. Add Micro block and allow mixing until the cement operation will finish.

    4- Before running Liner, check Plug Dropping Head assembly Specially Pump down Plug

        release Handle.

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03)                                             Page 122

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                       NIDC Drilling Projects Management
                              Drilling Engineering & Programming Division

                           South Azadegan Oil field

       NIDC                AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3                                                              PEDEC

    5- Before Liner shoe reach open hole (@ 9 5/8 shoe), prepare circulation at least one annular

      volume.

    6- When the Liner shoe reached the bottom ,connected Plug dropping head assembly then start

        circulation at least one annular volume.

    7- While Last circulation, prepare pre job safety meeting with involved personnel and arrange

       with mud engineer to measure drilling fluid & cement loss/gain during cement job.

    8- Stop circulation, connect cement line.

    9-  Tests cement line W/5000 psi.

   10- Pump per-flush and spacer.

   11- Mix and pump cement slurry & pump it in the well.

   12- Release pump down plug.

   13- Displace With mud at optimum rate. Slow pump rate 10 Bbl before Pump down Plug reaches

      Wiper Plug (Drill Pipe Displacement is ……. Bbl) at +/- …….Psi.

   14- Watch for indication of drill pipe plug latching into liner, Wiper Plug on pressure gauge. If

        this pressure indication is seen, recalculate total displacement volume. The new displacement

      volume will be the actual displacement volume for drill pipe plus the calculated liner volume.

   15- Slow pump rate down to 3 bbl/ minute, last 10 bbl of displacement volume.

   16- Land Plug with 1000 psi over final displacement pressure.

   Note:  If plug does not bump, do not displace more than %25 of the calculated shoe track.

    (Optional)

   17- Bleed off pressure and monitor U-Tube return.

   18- Monitoring return line for checking Float Collar and Float Shoe.

   19- Pick up liner hanger running tool and circulate on top of liner hanger, dump excess cement

      and mixture until clean.

   21- Rig down cement line.

     TRSCSSSV, and close SSV and Christmas tree on surface.

4  Kill and Choke Manifold Sketch

5  Pressure Test

      5.1 Accumulator, BOP, Wellhead and Choke/Kill Manifold

   The BOP, wellhead, choke manifold, and related equipment are normally to be tested on 14
       days basis. This period can be extended depending on the well operations and the
        specifications in the Drilling Program and the applicable Regulations.

   The accumulator is to be tested prior to the first test of the BOP, or any time after repairs are
      made to the accumulator unit. The accumulator bottles pre-charge pressure shall be checked
       before drilling out the casing shoe or every 4 weeks.

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03)                                             Page 132

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                       NIDC Drilling Projects Management
                              Drilling Engineering & Programming Division

                           South Azadegan Oil field

       NIDC                AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3                                                              PEDEC

    All the pressure tests shall be performed with both low pressure test, usually 300 psi and high
       pressure test, usually to the rated value (for annular BOP is 70% of WP) or as per Drilling
       Program. All high pressure tests should be maintained for 10-30 minutes, and low pressure
         test 5 minutes, unless the Drilling Program dictates otherwise.

      5.2 Casing and Liner
In the below table, casing or liner integrity test pressure, surface SIP, is given when test medium is
fresh water. As usual, green test casing integrity after bump plug is recommended in the cement
procedure. If rigs use the displacement mud (density >1.0sg) to test casing, the pressure applied on
surface cement head should be considered the mud hydrostatic pressure at casing collar And the
surface SIP is given as the following formula:
Surface shut-in Pressure = 80% Casing Burst – B (psi)
B=Drilling fluid hydrostatic pressure - fresh water hydrostatic pressure
Notes: the assumption in the formula above is the external fluid of the casing is fresh
water(density:1.0sg), in order to simply the calculation the test fluid inside casing is also assumed as

       NIDC                AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3                                                              PEDEC

fresh water(density:1.0sg), the actual test pressure at well site need to be recalculated and are
subject to the real mud weight in hole.

      5.3 Wellhead
    For port seal: Install a test pump to the port and inject test fluid and test pressure to which is
        less than 80% of casing collapse. Hold and monitor the test pressure for 15 minutes. In the
       event of a leak, remove the Wellhead and replace the seals as required. Once a satisfactory test
         is achieved, carefully bleed off all test pressure, drain the test fluid and reinstall the dust cap.

    For flange seal: Flange seal and body integrity test will be conducted at the same time when
       pressure test for ram BOP together

      5.4 LOT/FIT
   LOT will be performed in the first well in every area and in all appraisal wells to determine
       the equivalent mud weight at which the formation will take fluid. LOT will be conducted after
         drill out 13-3/8″, 9-5/8″ and 7″ shoe track to the first weak zone, especially the sand zone, but
      no more than 10 m in the new section hole.

                       NIDC Drilling Projects Management
                              Drilling Engineering & Programming Division

                           South Azadegan Oil field

       NIDC                AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3                                                              PEDEC

   FIT will be performed in the other wells. The test is not designed to breakdown or fracture
       formation, just to test the capacity of formation strength. Shut in pressure in wellhead during
      FIT should be the value that the hydrostatic pressure at casing shoe equals to (max mud
       weight of this hole section+2 PCF)

6  Well Control during Special operation

The well control while Wireline logging and running casing is important and critical, best practices
shall be followed, including but not limited to:

1) Change BOP ram to casing size and pressure test same to 1500 psi of surface SIP or the value of
  80% casing burst with fresh water.

2) The crossover for casing connection to drill pipe must available on floor. A good practice is to
   keep a joint of DP with casing crossover at lower end and full opened safety valve at upper end to
   be ready for picking up on V-door.

3)  Prior to Wireline logging, install shooting nipple and pressure test same to again WL BOP to
   1500 psi.

4) The cable cutter should be available on rig floor. If kick occurs, cut logging cable and to close
   blend BOP is optional

5) The contractor shall perform relative pressure test, drills, safety checks etc as per NIOC/NIDC
    Policy during the well operations.

1  26” HOLE SIZE
Description:
The 26” hole is planned to drill with Bentonite Hi Vis. Mud to Section TD at 150 m to run 20”
surface casing.
After drilling of each stand, 40 bbl. of High viscous pill should be pumped to clean hole or more if
the hole condition dictates. Once TD is reached, pump 80 bbl. of high viscous pill, circulate it out and
prior to pull out the BHA for running the 20" casing,
General practices to help avoiding hole to pack off during connection:
      Circulate out the whole with 100 bbl. Hi-Vis pill prior to any Totco run in hole
      Prior to POOH for BHA change, spot sufficient amount of Hi-Vis pill at bottom

2  17 ½” HOLE SIZE
Description:
The 17 1/2” hole section will be drilled from 20" casing shoe at 150 ± m to an estimated depth of
1449± m. The interval consists of two formations, Aghajari, and Gachsaran (5m above Asmari
formation). The formations are predominately made up of Sandstone, Clay stone, Marl, and
anhydrate.
This section will be drilled down using bentonite mud to 300 m from previous section. After drilling
300 m interval, mud should be dumped and the hole will be displaced with KCl / Polymer system and
drilling continues to the depth of 1449m (MD).

7  Notices
    1) Ensure the cement quality of corral pit & treatment area and the isolation of waste pit & clear

         pit using geo membrane before drilling the well.

    2) Ensure  the  existence and  safety of  all equipment & machinery  for  control  of waste

      management.

    3) The cement slurry inside cellar after well cementing should be transferred to the corral pit and

      mixing with drilling cutting.

    4) The produced waste due to washing the cement track mixer shall be transferred to the corral

         pit.

    5) Avoid using too much water for washing the mud pumps, tanks and the rig site.

    6)  Tipper truck should be checked in order to prevent any fluid leakage.

    7) Mud and waste management engineers should cooperate in some operations like washing the

     mud tanks and using diaphragm pumps.

    8) 50% of recycled water should be used for making drilling fluid and the remainder will be used

        to wash the equipment and rig site and etc.

    9) Mud and waste management engineers should fill in the forms related to equipment according

        to fluid properties and work hour of equipment.

   10) Record the fluid volume which is made daily.

   11) Record the dewatered fluid volume used for daily mud making and daily washing separately.

   12) Produced Solids must be fixed using Gypsum (except solids of 26"& first part of 17 1/2"Holes

        sections)

   13) Carry out quality control tests on fixed cuttings (sheen test & can test)

   14) Transfer fixed solids to disposal area at minimum possible time with regard to the prevention

       of environmental pollution.

1 Basic HSE Requirements

  1.  Contractor should establish an effective HSE system and implement it strictly.

  2.  Contractor must adhere to the standards and regulations of Health, Safe and Environment constituted by

      the local government.

  3.  Contractor must strictly adhere to the procedures  / standards and regulations of Health, Safety and

     Environment promulgated by NIDC.

  4.  Contractor should comply with all applicable local government regulations including guidelines and

    recommended practices and any other Iranian authorities having jurisdiction.

  5.  All personnel should respect the local culture, traditions and social values of the indigenous population of

      the Iran and South Azadegan region.

  6.  Contractor shall hire qualified and experienced worker and familiar his personnel with hazards especially

     with H2S and Mine and UXO (unexploded ordinance) according to related procedure.

  7.  Contractor shall conduct proper training about UXO (unexploded ordinance) and mine awareness to all

     personnel

2 Well Control

All the drilling operation shall obey the requirement of Well Control Manual of NIDC and

contractor’s well control manual; under no circumstances should the deviation from well control

policy be permitted without prior approval through NIDC. If the contractor’s well control manual or

anywhere has conflict with Well Control Manual of NIDC, take Well Control Manual of NIDC as the

standard.

(1) RECORDING REFERENCE

All BOP tests, drills, function tests, any malfunctions, repair or maintenance to the mud system and well

control equipment shall be recorded in the IADC daily reports and shall be signed by both the Drilling

Contractor's Tool Pusher and Drilling Supervisor on the well site. They shall also be recorded in the Daily

Drilling Report.

(2) FLOW CHECK PROCEDURE

To avoid a kick situation, it is recommended that a flow check be conducted at specific times during the

drilling operation.

1)  While drilling(any warning sign)

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03)                                             Page 146

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                       NIDC Drilling Projects Management
                              Drilling Engineering & Programming Division

                           South Azadegan Oil field

       NIDC                AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3                                                              PEDEC

2)   Prior to tripping the drill string

3)  While tripping(any warning sign)

4)  While out of hole(continuously)

5)  Trip out:

     After pulling 5% of the drill pipe

    At the midpoint depth of the wellbore

     Prior to pulling the first stand of drill collars

     After all the drill string is out of hole.

6)  Trip in:

    Upon reaching surface casing shoe

    At midpoint of the wellbore

(3) PRIMARY CONTROL REFERENCE

It is the responsibility of the Drilling Contractor to keep the hole full at all times by using all the available

equipment. The Drilling Supervisor shall monitor that the correct preventive practices are being implemented

and/or conducted at all times.

Extreme care shall be taken to monitor mud volume, drilling breaks and gas cut mud.

The MACP (Maximum allowable casing pressure) shall be confirmed by the Drilling Supervisor, either after

setting each new casing string or, whenever the density of the drilling mud changes.

The MACP shall be clearly written on a kick control sheet that will be posted near the choke control panel.

The predetermined distance at which the Tool Joint is to be pulled above the rotary should be posted on rig

floor near the BOP control panel.

The Driller is responsible for carefully measuring and recording the slow pump pressure. Awareness of these

values is an important element in killing operations, in order to avoid formation breakdown.

Take a measure and record the slow pump pressure at three different flow rates with all pumps at the following

times:

    Once per tour.

    Whenever changes occur in the mud density/rheology.

    Whenever changes occur in the dimension and characteristics of the string, BHA, jet size, jet plugged

         or jet lost, etc.

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03)                                             Page 147

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                       NIDC Drilling Projects Management
                              Drilling Engineering & Programming Division

                           South Azadegan Oil field

       NIDC                AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3                                                              PEDEC

(4) SECONDARY CONTROL REFERENCE

Any time a drilling break is noticed, drilling shall stop and a static monitoring of the well shall be carried out.

Space-out for all Pipe Rams and Annular Preventer must be known to the Driller at all times to keep tool joints

clear of annular preventer and rams.

(5) KICK DETECTION PROCEDURE WHILE DRILLING

If there is an indication of a possible kick occurring, the Driller shall follow the kick detection procedures

outlined below:

Stop drilling with the pump onPick up top drive to a predetermined space-out positionShut off the pumps

and check for well flow by visual examination on the bell nipple or flow line.

1)   If the well is not flowing:

    Immediately notify the rig manager, Drilling Supervisor and Tool pusher.

    The Drilling Supervisor will decide whether to circulate bottoms up or resume  drilling. The

         penetration rate will be limited as long as the cause of the abnormal situation is undetermined.

        Always be aware that some hydrocarbons (gas or oil cut mud) may rise to the surface. Ensure that the

         proper monitoring and safety systems all in place.

2)   If the well is flowing:

    Close the well as per the Shut-in Procedure.

    Immediately notify the Well site Drilling Supervisor, rig manager and Tool pusher.

3 Health
   1.  This well is committed to develop for oil in a responsible manner that promotes employees'

      and local inhabitants' health.

   2. A trained Paramedic and proper MEDIVAC equipment and ambulance shall be stationed on

       the drilling rig during the drilling campaign.

   3. An emergency medical evacuation plan shall be coordinated, and shall keep in touch with

     emergency response centres manned by qualified doctor 24-hours per day in Ahwaz city.

   4.  Medical emergency report and investigation  shall be performed as soon as possible in

      accordance with Medical Emergency Procedures.

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03)                                             Page 148

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                       NIDC Drilling Projects Management
                              Drilling Engineering & Programming Division

                           South Azadegan Oil field

       NIDC                AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3                                                              PEDEC

4 Safety

        1.  Contractor  shall make  detail  operation procedures which have covered  all  related

             activities.

        2. Due to high concentration of H2S in Sarvak, Kazhdumi and Gadvan reservoirs, Make
            detail H2S operation procedures, in particular, and carry it out strictly.

         3.  Contractor must provide lifesaving equipment like SCBA, Escape Mask, H2S detectors (Multi
            portable and personal detector) and implement the H2S Services system during drilling activity.
         4.  All lifting equipment and cranes must be certified by approved 3rd party (approved by Institute of

           Standards & Industrial Research of Iran)
       5.  All electrical equipment should be designed for such locations, and listed by nationally recognized
             testing laboratory. All wiring components and electrical equipment should be maintained in

           accordance with the manufacturers’ recommendation.

         6.  Contractor shall implement the risk assessment and Job Safety Analysis before starting the drilling

              activity.

         7.  PSSR (Pre-Start up Safety Review) before spud-in and penetrating into oil and gas formations are

          mandatory by contractor under the supervision of Company and approved by Company.
       8.  Contractor shall pay much attention to fire, explosion prevention and well control.
        9.  Contractor shall make detail Emergency Response Plan and Evacuation Plan.

       10. Contractor shall do efforts to keep employees safety as possible.

5 Environment

Requirement on location

1)  The access road and well location construction shall be accorded with construction program of

    well location. It minimizes any impacts on the local residents, prevent all soil contamination,

    prevent all ground water contamination, and prevent all surface water contamination. Cutting and

    waste water pit shall be lining and drainage system shall be good and to avoid farm and season

    canal contamination.

2)  Management regulation for transportation truck, drilling operation machine and tools shall be

     carried out strictly to avoid damage farm and trees, etc.

3)  Well site shall be flat and clean. All the equipment, materials, drilling tools shall be put in order.

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03)                                             Page 149

4)  Control the rig noise to decrease the influence to surroundings while drilling at the inhabitation

     area.
5)  Contractor shall perform on‐site segregation of wastes prior to transfer to the transfer area and/or

     delivery  to  the  transportation Contractor  in accordance with  the following waste hazard

     classification.

Non-Hazardous Wastes (Re-usable, Recyclable and Recoverable Wastes)

1)  Ferrous and non-ferrous metals, plastics, and wood wastes, PETs and potentially paper and glass

2)  Materials shall be separately segregated and provision may be made for size reduction and

    containerization equipment.

3) The Contractor/Generators are encouraged to seek for recycle/re-use alternatives based on the

   Local available facilities, for final disposal of these materials.

4)  It should be noted that landfill disposal of metal waste materials and wood wastes shall be

    prohibited. For wood wastes re-use of and/or salvage of commercial timber, or deliver/release to

5) Local community are recommended.

Hazardous Wastes

Hazardous Waste, such as clinic waste, oil waste, waste produced by painting, insulation works shall be duly

segregated.

Facilities of environment protection supplied to well site

1) Mud purifying circulation system

2) Cooling water spraying and circulating system for drilling pump

3)  Special recovery tank for deposed oil

4) Reserve house for valuable mud chemicals

5) Automatic mud weighting supply system

6) BOP stack is equipped according to drilling design

7) Define a separation area for gathering all waste according to waste management procedure

                           South Azadegan Oil field

Requirement of environment protection on location
1) Sewage pit Area of the sewage pit: 1500 m2

2)  Prior to spud, mud pit and sewage pit must comply with the standard. Install cooling water

   spraying and circulating system for drilling pump and sewage circulating system for cleaning rig

    floor and equipment.

3)  Install BOP stack and pressure test shall be operated according to the drilling design to avoid

   blowout.

4)  Install oil supply lines and check whether there is any leakage.

Management of environment protection on location

    1)  In drilling operation， keep mud and sewage from overflowing. If it happens, clean up timely.

    2) Mud chemicals shall be stored according to the standard and shall not be scattered around well

         site. If mud chemicals scatter while loading or unloading, clean it up at once. The sack of mud

       chemicals shall be recovered. Do not dispose it on site.

    3) Enhance water management at well location and do not wash equipment with fresh water.

       Stop water flowing for long time. Overhead tanks shall be utilized.

    4)  Oil management shall be carefully at the well site. No oil dirt on site, no dripping and leaking

        at oil tank and manifold system area.

    5)  After the well is completed, reinforce the sewage pit， solidify the disposed mud and have

       environment procedure fulfilled.

    6)  Install fuel supply lines and keep them in good condition.

    7)  All the drilling fluid materials shall be recovered and shall not be left at the well site after

       completion.

    8) The environmental protection personnel from contractor and operator side shall check and

       accept the well site environment according to the regulation after completion. Only well site

       environment is qualified, the well can be delivered.

    9)  Transition Area for Hazardous Waste shall also be provided by each Contractor in his

        allocated area,

      Company shall approve each area before use as well. The transition area of hazardous waste shall

        be:

      Paved, fenced, marked and illuminated

     Equipped with appropriate Fire Extinguisher and other emergency response equipment

      (e.g. Eye-Wash and Emergency Shower, appropriate First Aid kits, neutralizer substances

              etc.).

      In compliance with the requirements of handling, transportation and storage of hazardous

          waste (based on usage chemical MSDS).

      Configured to accommodate a safe physical separation of incompatible waste materials

             (e.g. acids, and alkalis, organic materials and strong oxidizing agents).

      Capable enough to provide storage for the entire range of hazardous wastes generated

          during the work.

     The area shall be provided with Spill Containment and means of preventing run‐off to and

         from the area.

      All waste containers to be stored shall be appropriately labeled.

     Hazardous Waste shall be transferred in a timely manner, within a maximum period of 48

           hours, for any period in excess of this permission shall be granted from Company and/or

        PEDEC.

Standard of utilization and discharging of wastes on location

1) Conduct drilling operation according to the design, drilling mud is needed before spud.

2) Sewage that cannot be recovered shall be put into the pit， do not discharge. If the volume of

   sewage is too big and some should be discharged， the quality of drainage must comply with the

   following regulations.

                 Maximum permitted concentration of wastes in water

                                                                                    Concentration
      Number     Items       Concentration    Number          Items
                                                                                 (mg/L)

         1       pH        6～9          4       Volatilization powder          0.5

         2      Crude oil         10           5             Sulfide               1

                   Floating                            Oxygen consumed by
         3                      100           6                               100
                     objects                                    Chemicals

3)  Make full use of waste water. Water in mud pump and water brake must be utilized by

      circulating. Water that is used to wash drill floor, shall be recovered after deposition. The

     recovery rate of waste water shall reach 85％.

4)   Disposed oil shall be recovered entirely. Recovery rate shall be 100％.Dumping of waste oil

      shall be prohibited.

5)   In drilling operation, non-chrome thinner high temperature mud system shall be used often if

     engineering is qualified.

6)   While drilling in special area， adopt mud creation inhabitation techniques to decrease the waste

    mud creation.

7)   After completion, all the mud chemicals, bentonite, clays shall be recovered and shall not be left

      at the well site. Mud in purifying tank shall also be recovered after completion.

8)   In case of any flammable or noxious gas encountered， steps shall be taken at once to prevent

     kicks and blowout. Gas shall be guided into ignition equipment to burn it away.

9)   Burning material that may result in serious smog or horrible smell at the well site is not allowed.

     For cement or barite that may result in granular dust pollution， operation shall be performed in

     a closed system.

10)  For abandoned well， filled with cement plugs to seal the oil, gas or water formation.

11)  Contractor/Generators are required to transport all generated wastes to the location/facilities

     designated, and licensed by authorities and/or approved/verified by the Company/PEDEC (as

       appropriate).

12)   All wastes shall be transferred by approved Sub‐Contractor (which could be a 3rd party Sub-

     Contractor of the Contractor/Generator itself). The Waste Transporter and any other waste

    management service Contractor shall be licensed in accordance with local regulations and are in

     compliance with such regulations. Company shall verify and approve the Waste Transportation

     Contractor by checking licenses and qualifications, audit and inspections, verification of human

      resources and received trainings etc.

13)  The transportation Contractor  shall ensure  that  all wastes are transported in a safe and

     responsible manner.

6 H2S monitoring and prevention measure

    6.1  Monitoring H2S
             1) Somewhere on the well site is apt to accumulate H2S, such as mud pit, shale shaker,
              and drilling platform, should be installed one set of. Make sure H2S monitor and

               alarm system can be used.

             2) Alarm threshold  setting  for H2S warning  indicator: the  first warning  level  is
              10mg/m3, unable warning sound,  just show present value of H2S content  to
                 operators. The second warning level is 20mg/m3, when the H2S content in air exceed
             20mg/m3, H2S warning indicator can give an alarm automatically, and warning

              sound should be heard by everyone work in well site. The audible alarm should be

                  installed on Kelly board.

             3) Equip enough gas masks on drilling platform, shaker screen (total amount should

                 large than 24), and equip 12 sets of 30min positive pressure Air Aspirators，2 sets of

                 portable H2S monitor and appropriative H2S warning indicator. Arrange a person

                 specially assigned for taking charge all the protective apparatus. This man must do

                 periodical check to keep these equipment in a good condition.

             4) H2S monitor should be done weekly check and compulsory examination.
             5)  After drilling into the pay zone, strengthen the detection of H2S content in mud.

Drilling Program WELL NO. AZNS# 045 (F 03)                                             Page 154

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                       NIDC Drilling Projects Management
                              Drilling Engineering & Programming Division

                           South Azadegan Oil field

       NIDC                AZNS#045 (F 03 – Area II ) Rev 0.3                                                              PEDEC

    6.2  Measures for H2S prevention

             1) Windsocks shall be set on the  drill floor and in the monsoon direction or/and

                 prevailing wind  direction of Azadegan  oil  field,  off well  site.  In emergency,

              employees must follow the evacuation procedure.

             2)  Explosion-proof blowers shall be installed around the rig, shale shaker, and drilling

                  fluid tanks to blow away toxic and flammable gas at the well site.

             3) The rig crew in the H2S area shall be furnished with H2S monitors and adequate

                 protective apparatus. Everybody shall be able to use, maintain, and check the

                 apparatus.

             4) The technicians of rig crew are responsible for H2S proof training and the tool

               pusher is in charge of the supervision and examination.

             5) The pH value of drilling fluid in the H2S area shall be kept over 9.5. Pay attention to
              measurement of H2S concentration in drilling fluid. The H2S concentration shall be
                kept at less than 50mg/cm3 by using sulphur removal agents and degassers.

             6)  Personnel safety and protection shall be carried out according to SY/T5087 during

                operations in polluted areas where H2S content exceeds the critical concentration.

             7) When the rig crew makes decision to bleed and fire because well control cannot be

               performed due to well site conditions, operations shall be performed according to

               procedures in SY/T5087.

             8) Once blowout occurs, the incident shall be reported to the higher level department as

              soon as possible and firefighting trucks, ambulances, medical care personnel and

                 technicians shall be on duty at the well site.

             9)  After the blowout is controlled, H2S concentration should be measured in the places
              where H2S is possible to gather.
            10) The personnel are not allowed to access until the H2S concentration is below the

                    critical concentration.
